# PART XVIII — FAILURE ECONOMICS

## XVIII.1 The chain, and why every failure gets all seven steps

**[P]** Bound by task §15. **A failure mode that is only described is a failure mode that will be improvised. Every one of the eighteen carries the full chain: `DETECTION → CONTAINMENT → DECISION → MONEY IMPACT → RESPONSIBILITY → RECOVERY → RECORD`.**

| Step | **What it must answer** | **Constraint** |
|---|---|---|
| **DETECTION** | **What observable fact proves the failure has occurred?** | Must be computable from a record the architecture already requires. **A detection that needs new data is not a detection — it is a hope** |
| **CONTAINMENT** | **What stops immediately?** | Automatic where possible. **Containment never requires a meeting** |
| **DECISION** | **Who decides the next act, and under what authority?** | A named human role. **AI detects; humans decide — HG-1 … HG-7, Part VIII** |
| **MONEY IMPACT** | **Which pool, which priority, which person is affected?** | Stated by pool and priority, never by amount |
| **RESPONSIBILITY** | **Who is accountable — as distinct from who is blamed?** | **Accountability follows authority. Where authority is unresolved, responsibility is `REQUIRES GOVERNANCE DECISION`, and it does not default to the person who noticed** |
| **RECOVERY** | **How does the system return to a lawful, funded state?** | **Recovery may not transfer cost to members personally — `D-3`, X-8** |
| **RECORD** | **What is written down, and who may read it?** | The relevant record type of Part XXI, with all eight fields. **Visible to the people it affects** |

## XVIII.2 Failure modes F-01 … F-09

| # | **Failure mode** | **DETECTION** | **CONTAINMENT** | **DECISION** | **MONEY IMPACT** | **RESPONSIBILITY** | **RECOVERY** | **RECORD** |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **F-01** | **Zero collection in a period** | `V_collected = 0` for a venture in a period, with `V_invoiced` stated separately (**RR-2**) | **All discretionary allocation stops. No new commitment is authorised. **The waterfall does not operate — there is nothing to sequence***** | **Team Leader + AI & Business Manager, human, same period** | **Priority 1 falls to zero. **The joint tier is unfunded. Priorities 3–9 are void***** | **The authority that approved the venture — not the worker who delivered into it** | **Runway assessment → restructure, pause or close (**`LC-2`, **HG-5***) → the shortfall rule §XI.5 **only after** SS-0's labour-law gate | **Venture record + period reconciliation + accrual per AC-1 … AC-5** |
| **F-02** | **Direct costs exceed the collection** | `R_3 > S_2` at the close of a period, per venture | **The venture stops taking new work. **`HG-4` withdraws approval for further commitments until the gap is closed***** | **AI & Business Manager proposes; human gate HG-5 decides: restructure, pause or close** | **Priority 3 unpaid → creditors, materials, subcontractors. **Priorities 4–9 unfunded***** | **The proposer of the venture and the approver of its cost estimate, jointly** | **Reprice, reduce scope, shorten the collection cycle, or close. **Never by deferring priority 1***** | **Venture record + cost record + decision record with the reason and the date** |
| **F-03** | **Customer default after delivery** | An acceptance exists, an invoice exists, and no collection exists beyond the stated cycle | **Collection effort is recorded and bounded; the accrual is created immediately, not at period end** | **Funds Manager detects; **AI may not write off, forgive or pursue**; a human decides escalation** | **Priority 1 exposure: **verified work performed, no money to pay for it — the corpus's "structural problem"***** | **The organisation, not the worker. **The worker's claim survives the customer's default — AC-1, AC-4***** | **Accrual + next-collection settlement at priority 1 + customer concentration review (**RR-4**, **`G-10`***) | **Customer record + acceptance + accrual + ageing** |
| **F-04** | **Collection delayed beyond the compensation cycle** | `V_invoiced > V_collected` by more than the stated cycle (**RR-1**, **RR-2**) | **The cadence may be adjusted; **the priority order and the transparency obligation may not** — absolute rule 3** | **Human, at the level that owns the cadence; **National approval if the cadence is a member-facing promise** (**MD-02***) | **Priority 1 timing. **Not a reduction — a delay, and a delay is a real cost to a person without reserves***** | **The authority that set the cycle and the accounting basis** | **Disclose the delay before it happens (**`D-2`**), accrue, settle at priority 1 from the next collection | **Accrual + disclosure record + period reconciliation** |
| **F-05** | **A statutory obligation cannot be computed or met** | Priority 2's amount cannot be determined, or `S_1 < R_1 + R_2` | **The joint tier is protected as one (§X.5). **Nothing below priority 2 is paid. Statutory dues are never reduced by any rule** (**JT-3***) | **`REQUIRES PROFESSIONAL VALIDATION` — **and until it is obtained, the answer is to stop the activity that creates the obligation, not to guess at it***** | **Legal exposure, penalties, and the loss of the ability to operate at all** | **The organisation and its authorised signatory. **Not the Funds Manager personally — COI-1 forbids them from being the decider***** | **Professional instruction → computation → payment or cessation → record of the instruction relied on | **Statutory record + professional-advice record + decision record** |
| **F-06** | **The compensation formula is absent, unpublished, or changed retroactively** | A payment sheet exists with no formula version (**RT-16**), or a person was recruited before publication (**`D-1`**) | **Recruitment on the affected role stops immediately. **No further payment is made under an unpublished formula***** | **Human; the change requires publication with an effective date and may never apply backwards | **Priority 1 legitimacy. **An unpublished formula makes every payment discretionary, and discretionary payment is the mechanism by which loyalty is bought***** | **Whoever recruited or paid without a published formula** | **Publish → apply prospectively → recompute the affected period transparently → disclose the correction | **Formula record with version + payment sheet + publication record** |
| **F-07** | **The joint tier cannot be paid — worker compensation and mandatory dues both short** | `S_0 < R_1 + R_2` (**JT-1**, **JT-4**) | **Automatic: no lower-priority pool receives anything. **The waterfall *"should not divert money to lower-priority pools while verified worker compensation and mandatory dues remain unpaid"***** | **Human, same period. **SS-0 first: is partial payment lawful? If not, work is suspended, not underpaid***** | **The indivisible tier. Every person who delivered verified work, and every statutory obligation of the period** | **The authority that authorised the work without a funded joint tier — **which today is the same person who will feel it, and must still record it***** | **Pro rata to verified work value (**SS-1**), no self-exclusion (**SS-2**), published in the period (**SS-3**), accrued and settled at priority 1 (**AC-1**) | **Shortfall record + accrual + publication + escalation to the level above** |
| **F-08** | **A shock arrives before any reserve exists** | A named shock occurs while P-1's balance is zero (**XIV.4**) | **Contain the shock by stopping the activity that generates it. **There is no money to contain it with***** | **Human, with **`REQUIRES GOVERNANCE DECISION`** on any external source** | **Whichever pool the shock lands on — usually priority 1 or priority 3, because there is no buffer to absorb it first** | **The organisation. **The cold start is an architectural condition, not an individual's error***** | **P-6 float if a named source exists → non-cash resourcing (R-C) → pause or close. **Never a member contribution (**`D-3`***) | **Reserve record + shock record + decision record + the requirement named in writing** |
| **F-09** | **A reserve drawn without trigger, authority or return** | A draw exists with no stated trigger, no named authority, or no return position (**XIV.1**) | **The draw is reversed or frozen pending decision. **A reserve is not an operating account***** | **Human, at the level that owns the pool; **`REQUIRES GOVERNANCE DECISION` where the authority was never assigned***** | **P-1 … P-6 integrity. **An undisciplined reserve is worse than none, because it creates the belief that a buffer exists***** | **The person who signed the draw and the person who authorised it** | **Restore the pool, publish the event, and if the trigger was genuinely absent, **create the trigger by amendment rather than by precedent** | **Reserve record + authority record + audit finding** |

## XVIII.3 Failure modes F-10 … F-18

| # | **Failure mode** | **DETECTION** | **CONTAINMENT** | **DECISION** | **MONEY IMPACT** | **RESPONSIBILITY** | **RECOVERY** | **RECORD** |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **F-10** | **An unfunded mandate — a required role performed with no source** | A funding-map cell reads `NONE` while the role is being performed (**XII.2 FM-1, FM-2**) | **The obligation is not silently dropped. **It is either funded, formally suspended, or formally transferred — and the choice is published***** | **`REQUIRES GOVERNANCE DECISION` — **`H-Q17` is exactly this question***** | **L2 and the four `COI` roles. **Unfunded control functions are the ones that fail first and are missed last***** | **The architecture's authors and approvers — **not the person absorbing the cost invisibly***** | **Resolve `C_L1-a/b/c` → fund, reduce the mandate by amendment, or state openly that it is unfunded (**O-2***) | **Funding map with every cell filled + role record + decision record** |
| **F-11** | **Segregation failure — the same hands on both sides** | One person appears in two or more of propose / approve / sign / receive / authorise / verify / audit for the same transaction | **The transaction is void internally and stops. **Independence is not recoverable after the fact***** | **Human, at a level above the person involved. **Where no level above exists, `REQUIRES GOVERNANCE DECISION`***** | **Every pool. **A control system with one pair of hands is not a control system***** | **Whoever permitted or defaulted into the arrangement — **including this instrument, if it named no alternative***** | **Reassign, rotate, or bring in an external verifier; **never by adding a report that the same person writes***** | **Authority matrix + transaction record + the recusal or reassignment** |
| **F-12** | **Volunteer labour becomes an implicit obligation** | A person who declines a commercial assignment suffers any recorded consequence (**VB-2**), or unpaid work appears on a critical path | **The assignment is withdrawn. **The consequence is reversed and its reversal recorded***** | **Human; **Volunteer Manager detects but may not participate in the venture** — **`NM-10`***** | **HC-5, human time capital. **The cost is real and is being paid by the person least able to refuse***** | **Whoever assigned it, and whoever accepted the arrangement as normal** | **Publish the paid/volunteer boundary per role class (**VB-5**), measure the cost of volunteering (**`D-6`**), and convert by decision with a funding source (**VB-6***) | **Assignment record + decline record with no consequence + the boundary publication** |
| **F-13** | **Mission money used for a commercial cost, or commerce funded from mission money** | A mission-pool payment whose `WHY` is a venture cost, or a venture cost whose `AUTHORITY` is a restricted source (**X-1 … X-6**) | **Immediate stop and immediate reversal. **This is the crossing the whole flow architecture exists to prevent***** | **Human, at the highest level available; **a breach of BP-1 … BP-4 is a governance breach, not a performance matter***** | **Flow C/D integrity and the mission's own funding. **Also donor-restriction exposure, which is legal, not merely internal***** | **The authoriser and the recipient, jointly** | **Reverse → restore the pool → disclose to the members and to any donor whose restriction was touched → amend the control that failed | **Mission-instrument record with restriction as a field + reversal record + disclosure** |
| **F-14** | **The pre-surplus mission instrument is empty and service stops** | **`MI-1`**'s balance is zero while a mission obligation is open (**XIII.3**) | **The shortfall is escalated and published — **it does not stop silently**, because silence is how a mission organisation discovers it has become a business** | **Human; **`REQUIRES GOVERNANCE DECISION`** on source** | **L4. **And, indirectly, the organisation's reason to exist, which is not a pool but is the only thing members cannot be paid to replace***** | **The authority that accepted a mission obligation without funding it** | **Restricted donation honoured to its restriction → non-cash resourcing → governed contribution (**Part XVI**) → **or say plainly that the service cannot be offered this period***** | **Mission-instrument record + shortfall record + publication + escalation** |
| **F-15** | **A recruitment-linked economic benefit appears anywhere** | Any compensation, bonus, allocation, priority, recognition or access that varies with any count of people (**`MLM-1`**, **T1**) | **The benefit is void and stops immediately. **This is a veto-gate breach, not a finding to be remediated in the next cycle***** | **Human, at the highest level available. **No AI classification, no officer waiver***** | **Every flow. **Once a count of people has economic value, the organisation's growth incentive becomes its members' recruitment, and the mission is the marketing***** | **Whoever designed, approved or tolerated it — **including anyone who called it "just recognition"***** | **Remove the link → run **T1** as a correlation report against all compensation records → publish the result → amend the control that permitted it | **Compensation record + recruitment attribution + the **T1** report + the amendment** |
| **F-16** | **A related-party transaction outside RP-1 … RP-8** | A transaction meeting any of RP-T1 … RP-T5 with no prior register entry, no recorded recusal, or no disclosure (**RP-8**) | **Void as an internal matter and stopped, irrespective of its legal status** | **Human, with the interested person recused from every step. **`CF-16` cannot be managed by the person it concerns***** | **Whichever pool was touched, and the legitimacy of every adjacent decision** | **The interested person and the authoriser** | **Register retrospectively with the reason it was missed → reverse or ratify on arms-length evidence (**RP-3**, **`REQUIRES PROFESSIONAL VALIDATION`**) → disclose to affected members (**RP-4***) | **Related-party register + recusal record + disclosure record + audit finding** |
| **F-17** | **Records absent, incomplete or unverifiable** | Any of the 17 record types missing one of the eight fields, or a payment whose evidence chain cannot be followed (**Part XXI**) | **The affected activity stops. **An unverifiable transaction is treated as unauthorised, not as probably fine***** | **Human; **the auditor detects, and may not clear, cure or classify***** | **Every pool, because every pool is disbursed on the strength of a record** | **The person who created the record and the person who relied on it** | **Reconstruct from primary evidence where it exists; **where it does not, record the absence as an absence — Rule K-5, `NO SOURCE → NO FACT → NO CLOSURE`***** | **Record with all eight fields + an explicit gap statement where reconstruction failed** |
| **F-18** | **AI performs, or appears to perform, a consequential economic act** | Any pricing, contract formation, credit, collection, expense classification, reserve draw, related-party clearance or venture closure whose `AUTHORITY` is not a named human (**Part VIII's matrix**) | **The act is void and reversed. **Containment is automatic and does not wait for a review***** | **Human gate HG-1 … HG-7. **AI may detect, draft, model, summarise and flag; it may never decide***** | **Whichever pool the act touched, plus the accountability of every person who relied on the output** | **The human who delegated, or who failed to notice the delegation. **Never the system***** | **Reverse → re-decide by a named human → record both the AI output and the human decision separately → amend the boundary that was crossed | **AI-output record + human-decision record, **kept apart so that the human decision is always attributable***** |

## XVIII.4 What the eighteen have in common

| Pattern | **Content** |
|---|---|
| **1** | **Fourteen of the eighteen are detected from a record the architecture already requires.** Only F-05, F-08, F-11 and F-14 need a fact from outside the ordinary record set — and each of those four is the reason the corresponding register exists |
| **2** | **Not one is contained by spending money.** Containment is always a *stop*. **This is the only failure architecture that works at zero capital** |
| **3** | **Not one is decided by AI.** Eighteen modes, eighteen human decisions |
| **4** | **Eleven place responsibility on an authority rather than on an individual** — because in a standing-start organisation the person who absorbs a failure is usually the person with least power to prevent it |
| **5** | **Not one is recovered by asking a member to pay.** `D-3` holds across all eighteen |
| **6** | **All eighteen are recorded in a form the affected members can read.** A failure that is recorded only upward is a failure that will recur |
| **7** | **Six are already live today:** F-05 *(statutory obligations cannot be computed)*, F-08 *(no reserve exists)*, F-10 *(eight of fourteen funding-map rows are `NONE`)*, F-11 *(eleven people, no segregation available)*, F-14 *(no mission instrument exists)*, F-17 *(no records exist)*. **The architecture's present state is not "no failures" — it is six open failures and no revenue in which to hide them** |

---

# PART XIX — ANTI-GAMING ARCHITECTURE

## XIX.1 The governing rule

**[P]** Bound by **EA-21, EA-24**. **The corpus's gaming register `G-01 … G-16` is retained as the governing register **[S-B: §36.1]**. This Part specifies the fifteen vectors that arise specifically from the *current* economic architecture — the current roles, the current absence of layers, the current absence of software, and the current absence of money. Each is given a detection record, because a control that cannot be checked from a record is a preference.**

> ### **`A CONTROL THAT CAN ONLY BE VERIFIED BY THE PERSON IT CONSTRAINS IS NOT A CONTROL.`**
>
> **This is the test applied to all fifteen. Where the current architecture fails it — and with eleven people and no segregation available it fails it often — the failure is stated rather than papered over.**

## XIX.2 The fifteen vectors

| # | **Gaming vector** | **How it is actually played** | **Control** | **Detection record** | **State** |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **AG-01** | **Inflating verified work value** | A worker records their own hours, or a supervisor verifies a friend's deliverable, or "verification" becomes a formality performed by the person paid from it | **Verification is performed by a person who is not paid from the work verified** (**EL-2**, **COI-2**) · every verification names a verifier and a basis · the verifier may not be the beneficiary | **Verification record: who verified, on what evidence, on what date, and whether the verifier is paid from the same venture.** Generalises the corpus's **`G-07` ghost workers** and **`G-06` duplicate claims** **[S-B: §36.1]** | **`CONTROL SPECIFIED — SEGREGATION UNAVAILABLE TODAY: F-11 IS OPEN`** |
| **AG-02** | **Shifting costs between ventures and pools** | A venture's real cost is booked to another venture, to the organisation service fee, or to the mission instrument, so that the failing venture looks viable and the mission absorbs commerce | **Every cost carries a venture and a category** · **`G-05`** retained · RI-3's capitalisation route is explicitly noted as making this easier, which is why it is not adopted silently (**X.7**) · **X-1 … X-8** crossings prohibited | **Cost record with venture, pool, priority and category; a cost with no venture is an exception, not a default** | **`RETAINED + NEW`** |
| **AG-03** | **Recognising revenue that was never collected, or recording unpaid work as revenue** | An invoice becomes "revenue"; a favour becomes a "customer"; a member's free labour becomes a delivered project with a value attached | **No revenue without a customer, an acceptance and a delivery record** (**RR-6**, **`G-01`**) · `V_invoiced` and `V_collected` are separate fields and neither is used alone (**RR-2**) · unpaid work is never payable work (**EL-3**) | **Revenue record + acceptance record + reconciliation to bank or cash (**RT-18**)** | **`RETAINED + NEW`** |
| **AG-04** | **Timing manipulation across periods** | A collection is held to make a period look solvent, or pulled forward to make a shortfall disappear, or a cost is deferred into a period nobody will examine | **Period reconciliation is mandatory and closed on a stated date** · the highest solvency line reached is reported per period (**X.8**), which makes a moved collection visible in two periods instead of invisible in one · no period may be reopened without a recorded reason | **Period reconciliation + solvency-line report + any reopening with its reason and authority.** The corpus already names this vector: **`G-11` revenue timing manipulation** **[S-B: §36.1]** | **`RETAINED + NEW`** |
| **AG-05** | **Concentration disguised through intermediaries** | One customer's revenue is routed through two nominally separate buyers so that the concentration limit is never triggered | **Concentration is measured per ultimate customer, not per invoice** (**RR-4**, **`G-10`**) · the limit is `[M]` and is `REQUIRES GOVERNANCE DECISION` — it is never invented · related-party tests RP-T1 … RP-T5 apply to intermediaries | **Customer record with an ultimate-beneficiary field; a concentration report per period.** Extends **`G-10` customer-concentration hiding** and **`G-04` customer collusion** **[S-B: §36.1]** | **`RETAINED — LIMIT UNSET`** |
| **AG-06** | **Recruiting people to increase any allocation** | The oldest vector and the one that ends the organisation: headcount becomes the input to money, so growth is measured in people rather than in service | **`MLM-1`** — nothing is computed from any count of people · **`MLM-8`** — a venture whose revenue rises when membership doubles and customers do not is refused at G3 · **T1** run annually as a correlation report against all compensation records | **Compensation records against recruitment attribution; **T1**'s output is the entire assurance programme and it must be published.** Subsumes the corpus's **`G-13` volunteer-count inflation**, **`G-03` fake service activities** and **`G-08` artificial project completion** — all three are count-based **[S-B: §36.1]** | **`RETAINED — PERMANENT, NO APPROVAL ROUTE`** |
| **AG-07** | **Reconstructing a share of team output through the leader differential** | Not a percentage of revenue *(forbidden)* — so a percentage of compensation, or a "coordination fee" per person, or a bonus tied to team size, or an in-kind benefit that scales with the team | **`MLM-2`, `MLM-9`** · **LD-1 … LD-8** · the differential is justified by verified leadership **work** with its own record · the multiplier is `REQUIRES FOUNDER DECISION` and is never inferred | **Leadership-work record separate from delivery-work record; **MW-1 … MW-6** mixed-work recording makes the split auditable** | **`RETAINED + NEW (MLM-9)`** |
| **AG-08** | **Charging members indirectly** | A "voluntary" contribution that is visibly expected · materials sold "at cost" · a training fee called a donation · a kit that is technically optional and practically required | **`MLM-3`, `MLM-5`** — permanent, no approval route · **`MLM-10`** — no member-only terms and no venture whose principal market is its own members · **T4** — any receipt of money from a participant for participation is a veto-gate breach · **`D-3`** | **Every receipt into any pool, with the payer's relationship to the organisation as a field. **T4** is answered by one report** | **`RETAINED + NEW (MLM-10) — PERMANENT`** |
| **AG-09** | **Using mission access as an acquisition channel** | The service population becomes the customer list; help becomes a sales conversation; gratitude becomes a pipeline | **BP-1 … BP-5** · **X-5, X-6** · no mission role-holder may hold a commercial interest in a venture serving the population they serve (**BP-4**) | **Customer-acquisition source as a field on every customer record: a customer whose source is a mission contact is an exception requiring human review** | **`RETAINED (B-27) + NEW FIELD`** |
| **AG-10** | **Creating a claim on the organisation by funding it** | A contribution arrives, is welcomed, and quietly becomes a debt, an entitlement, a priority or a veto | **`AG-10`** capture before acceptance: amount, purpose, restriction, and **whether it creates any claim** · **RP-6** · **SEP-5** — contribution in is never netted against extraction out · a contribution may not be required (**`D-3`**) | **Contribution record with a "creates a claim" field answered explicitly, including when the answer is no** | **`NEW — NO CORPUS VECTOR EXISTS FOR IT`** |
| **AG-11** | **Reserve draws dressed as emergencies** | Every shortfall is an emergency; the pool exists on paper and is empty in practice; the trigger is written after the draw | **XIV.1** — named purpose, named authority, named trigger, named return · **F-09** treats an untriggered draw as a failure, not a judgement · the trigger is created by amendment, never by precedent | **Reserve record: trigger cited, authority named, return position stated, and the pool balance before and after** | **`RETAINED — AUTHORITY `REQUIRES GOVERNANCE DECISION``** |
| **AG-12** | **The seeded float becoming permanent income** | P-6 arrives, is spent on operating costs, is renewed, and the venture's real unit economics are never observed because the float was always there | **MD-05** — a named source, a cap, a term, **one renewal maximum**, and full reconciliation · **`G-15`** retained · the float is held separately from operating money (**XIV.1 segregation**) | **Float record: source, cap, term, draw, reconciliation, and the renewal count as an explicit field.** Retains **`G-15` float or reserve capture** and **`G-16` grant capture** — the corpus's own disclosure that *"B-04 creates a resource to be captured"* **[S-B: §36.1]** | **`NEW — PROPOSED; CONTAINS NO NUMBERS`** |
| **AG-13** | **Formula and version manipulation** | The formula changes and the change is applied to a period already worked, or the version that produced a payment cannot be identified, so no one can say whether it was correct | **RT-16** — every payment sheet carries the formula version · **AC-2** — an accrual carries the version that produced it · **F-06** treats a retroactive change as a failure · publication with an effective date, always prospective | **Formula record with version history + payment sheet + accrual, all carrying the version.** Also defeats **`G-06` duplicate claims** through the version-and-evidence chain | **`RETAINED + NEW (AC-2, F-06)`** |
| **AG-14** | **Routing a related-party transaction through a nominally unrelated counterparty** | The interested person does not appear; a relative, an associate, a venture or an intended entity does | **RP-T1 … RP-T5** define the counterparty by *influence*, not by name · **RP-1** prior registration · **RP-2** recorded recusal · **RP-3** arms-length evidence · **AI may flag the pattern but may never clear it** (**RP-7**) | **Related-party register + a connection field on every counterparty record + the recusal record** | **`NEW — CURRENT SEWAK ARCHITECTURE`** |
| **AG-15** | **Gaming the presentation of performance** | A score, a rank, a percentage, a badge or a league table is introduced, and behaviour optimises the measure instead of the service — the exact failure the corpus rejected | **J-18, J-22, MD-14** — no leaderboard, no ranking, no score · the only permitted performance statement is **the highest solvency line reached** (**X.8**), which is a fact about money, not a comparison between people · **EA-26** prohibits building any ranking instrument | **Period report containing the solvency line and nothing comparative between people or teams.** The corpus names it **`G-09` leaderboard gaming** **[S-B: §36.1]**; **J-18** and **MD-14** remove the instrument entirely rather than guard it | **`RETAINED REJECTION — PERMANENT`** |

## XIX.3 The meta-finding

| # | **Finding** |
|---|---|
| **1** | **Eleven of the fifteen vectors are controlled by a record the architecture already requires.** Four — AG-01, AG-05, AG-09, AG-14 — require a field that does not yet exist, and each is named above |
| **2** | **Three vectors are controlled permanently with no approval route: AG-06, AG-08, AG-15.** They are not risks to be managed; they are the organisation's identity, and an approval route for them would be an approval route for becoming something else |
| **3** | **`T1` and `T4` between them detect AG-06 and AG-08, which are the two vectors that would end the organisation.** Both are single reports computable from records already required. **The anti-MLM assurance programme is therefore two queries, not a department** |
| **4** | **Every control in this Part fails the governing rule today**, because with eleven people and no layers, the person constrained is often the only person available to verify. **This is `F-11`, and it is open.** The controls are still correct: they specify what must be true before the architecture may scale, and they name the failure that occurs if it scales without them |
| **5** | **No vector is controlled by software, automation, identity systems or dashboards.** **EA-26** prohibits building them and **§18 of the delta-audit brief** prohibits them absent an authorising gate. Every detection above is a record and a human reading it |

---

## XIX.4 Register reconciliation — `AG-01 … AG-15` against the corpus's `G-01 … G-16`

**[P]** Bound by **EA-2, EA-7**. **The corpus's gaming register is retained in full **[S-B: §36.1]**. This reconciliation exists because the two registers are not the same list, and because one correction is owed.**

| Corpus vector | **Retained here as** | **Note** |
|---|---|---|
| **`G-01` Fake revenue** | **AG-03** | The corpus's control is *"collected legitimate customer receipts"* — which is exactly **RR-2** and **RR-6** |
| **`G-02` Inflated expenses** | **AG-02** | Priority 3 reduces distributable amounts, so inflation is rewarded |
| **`G-03` Fake service activities** | **AG-06**, and **Part XIII** | Mission impact carries the highest weight and service is unfunded, *"so inflating it costs nothing"* |
| **`G-04` Customer collusion** | **AG-05, AG-09, AG-14** | Related-party exclusion is the corpus's own control |
| **`G-05` Expense shifting** | **AG-02** | *"The boundary between `C_var` and `C_L1`/`C_svc` is exactly where"* it lives — and **X.7 RI-3** would widen that boundary, which is why RI-3 is not adopted silently |
| **`G-06` Duplicate claims** | **AG-01, AG-13** | Payment sheet with formula version and evidence is the corpus's control, retained as **RT-16** |
| **`G-07` Ghost workers** | **AG-01** | Compensation is priority 1 and is paid on *verified work* |
| **`G-08` Artificial project completion** | **AG-06** | *"No artificial splitting"* — retained |
| **`G-09` Leaderboard gaming** | **AG-15** | **J-18, MD-14** remove the instrument rather than guard it |
| **`G-10` Customer-concentration hiding** | **AG-05** | **RR-4**; the limit remains `[M]` |
| **`G-11` Revenue-timing manipulation** | **AG-04** | Cut-off rules retained in **REC-16** |
| **`G-12` Under-reporting weakness** | **Part XVIII.1 `RESPONSIBILITY`, and XVIII.4 finding 6** | *"Nobody is punished for reporting a failure."* Every failure record in Part XVIII is visible to the people it affects, which is the structural form of the same rule |
| **`G-13` Volunteer-count inflation** | **AG-06** | **`CORRECTION OF RECORD:` an earlier draft of this Part cited `G-13` as "founder or insider contribution capture". It is not. The corpus's `G-13` is volunteer-count inflation. Contribution capture has no corpus vector and is therefore recorded as `NEW` at AG-10** |
| **`G-14` Evidence fabrication** | **AG-01, AG-03, F-17** | Evidence standards retained; **DR-4** — a reference to a document is not the document |
| **`G-15` Float or reserve capture** | **AG-12, AG-11** | The corpus's own disclosure: *"a risk this document itself introduces"* — retained with its mitigations |
| **`G-16` Grant capture** | **AG-12, XIII.2 R-B** | *"A grant is money without a customer."* Any restricted donation is exposed to it |
| **No corpus vector** | **AG-07** *(differential reconstruction)* · **AG-08** *(indirect member charging)* · **AG-10** *(contribution capture)* · **AG-13** *(formula version manipulation)* · **AG-14** *(related-party routing)* | **Five vectors are `NEW — CURRENT SEWAK ARCHITECTURE`. AG-07 and AG-08 are new because they are the evasions of `MLM-2` and `MLM-3` respectively, and a prohibition creates its own evasion route** |

> ### **`SIXTEEN CORPUS VECTORS RETAINED · FIFTEEN CURRENT-ARCHITECTURE VECTORS SPECIFIED · ELEVEN OVERLAP · FIVE ARE NEW · ONE CITATION CORRECTED OF RECORD.`**

# PART XX — ECONOMIC GOVERNANCE ARCHITECTURE

## XX.1 The eight governance functions

**[P]** Task §17 requires that, for every economic decision, the architecture state **who proposes, who approves, who signs, who receives, who authorises, who verifies, who calculates and who audits**. **The eight functions are defined first, because four of them are routinely collapsed into one person and the collapse is the failure.**

| Function | **What it is** | **What it is not** | **May it be combined?** |
|---|---|---|---|
| **1 — PROPOSES** | Originates the economic act and states its reason | Not the person who benefits from it | With 4 and 5 only under a recorded condition |
| **2 — APPROVES** | Confers authority for the act against a rule | **Not the proposer, and never the beneficiary** | **`NO — ABSOLUTE`** |
| **3 — SIGNS** | Executes the act in a form the outside world recognises | Not a formality: **today no one can sign, because no legal person exists (`SR-01`, `A-N16`)** | With 2 only if 2 is not the beneficiary |
| **4 — RECEIVES** | Takes custody of money or value | **Never the approver of their own receipt** | **`NO — COI-1`** |
| **5 — AUTHORISES** | Releases money or value from custody | **Never the same hands as 4** — the four-hand rule | **`NO — ABSOLUTE`** |
| **6 — VERIFIES** | Confirms that the work, the amount and the evidence are what they are claimed to be | **Not paid from what it verifies** | **`NO — COI-2, EL-2`** |
| **7 — CALCULATES** | Applies the formula and produces the number | **Not the person whose number it is** | With 6 only where no alternative exists, and then it is recorded as a segregation exception |
| **8 — AUDITS** | Examines after the fact, independently | **Detects; never cures, clears, classifies or waives** | **`NO — COI-2`** |

## XX.2 The assignment matrix

**[P]** Every economic act, against the eight functions. **`UNASSIGNED` means the architecture names no holder — which is a governance fact, not a drafting omission. `BLOCKED` means a holder may exist but cannot act.**

| Economic act | **Proposes** | **Approves** | **Signs** | **Receives** | **Authorises** | **Verifies** | **Calculates** | **Audits** |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Approve a venture** | AI & Business Manager | **`UNASSIGNED — no approval authority exists`** | Not applicable | Not applicable | Not applicable | Human gate HG-2/HG-3 | Not applicable | Internal Auditor |
| **Set a price** | AI & Business Manager | **`UNASSIGNED`** | Not applicable | Not applicable | Not applicable | Human gate HG-3 | Not applicable | Internal Auditor |
| **Accept a customer** | Ground-Level Manager | Team Leader *(within delegated authority)* | **`BLOCKED — SR-01`** | Funds Manager | **`BLOCKED — A-N16`** | HR & Verification | Not applicable | Internal Auditor |
| **Record verified work** | The worker | Team Leader | Not applicable | Not applicable | Not applicable | **HR & Verification — `MUST NOT BE PAID FROM IT`** | Not applicable | Internal Auditor |
| **Calculate compensation** | Not applicable | Not applicable | Not applicable | Not applicable | Not applicable | Not applicable | **`UNASSIGNED — the formula does not exist (§XI.3)`** | Internal Auditor |
| **Pay compensation** | Funds Manager | **`UNASSIGNED`** | **`BLOCKED — SR-01, A-N16`** | The worker | **`BLOCKED — A-N16`** | HR & Verification | As above | Internal Auditor |
| **Pay a statutory obligation** | Funds Manager | **`UNASSIGNED`** | **`BLOCKED`** | The authority | **`BLOCKED`** | **`REQUIRES PROFESSIONAL VALIDATION`** | **`REQUIRES PROFESSIONAL VALIDATION`** | Internal Auditor |
| **Draw a reserve** | Team Leader | **`UNASSIGNED — `REQUIRES GOVERNANCE DECISION``** | **`BLOCKED`** | Funds Manager | **`UNASSIGNED`** | Not applicable | Not applicable | Internal Auditor |
| **Draw the float P-6** | Team Leader | The named source's terms | **`BLOCKED`** | Funds Manager | **`UNASSIGNED`** | Not applicable | Not applicable | Internal Auditor |
| **Accept a contribution** | Any | **`UNASSIGNED`** | **`BLOCKED`** | Funds Manager | **`UNASSIGNED`** | Not applicable | Not applicable | Internal Auditor — **`AG-10` before acceptance** |
| **Approve founder compensation** | **Nobody may** | **`AUTHORITY UNRESOLVED (beneficiary recusal)` — `CF-16`** | **`BLOCKED`** | The founder | **`BLOCKED`** | Not applicable | Not applicable | External audit required — **internal independence is not achievable** |
| **Authorise a related-party transaction** | **The interested person may not** | **`UNASSIGNED — requires a disinterested approver who does not exist`** | **`BLOCKED`** | **`BLOCKED — RP-5`** | **`BLOCKED`** | **`BLOCKED`** | Not applicable | Internal Auditor |
| **Spend on mission activity** | Safety/Protection or Volunteer Manager | **`UNASSIGNED`** | **`BLOCKED`** | Funds Manager | **`UNASSIGNED`** | Not applicable | Not applicable | Internal Auditor |
| **Classify an expense** | The person incurring it | Funds Manager | Not applicable | Not applicable | Not applicable | **`AI MAY FLAG — A HUMAN DECIDES`** | Not applicable | Internal Auditor |
| **Close a venture** | AI & Business Manager | **`UNASSIGNED`** | **`BLOCKED`** | Not applicable | Not applicable | Human gate HG-5 | Not applicable | Internal Auditor |
| **Amend the waterfall** | Any | **National + professional validation + the founder's formal amendment — Tier-1** | **`BLOCKED — no National layer exists`** | Not applicable | Not applicable | Not applicable | Not applicable | Delta audit |

## XX.3 What the matrix shows

| # | **Finding** |
|---|---|
| **GF-1** | **The `APPROVES` column is `UNASSIGNED` in thirteen of sixteen acts.** The architecture has proposers, verifiers and auditors, and almost no approvers. **This is not a drafting gap; it is the absence of a governance layer, and it is the single largest obstacle to any economic activity whatsoever** |
| **GF-2** | **The `SIGNS` column is `BLOCKED` in every act that involves money.** `SR-01` and `A-N16` together mean that no economic act in this architecture can currently be executed. **Sixteen acts, zero executable** |
| **GF-3** | **The `CALCULATES` column is empty for compensation, because the formula does not exist.** Everything downstream of it — payment sheets, accruals, version control, T1 — is therefore also empty |
| **GF-4** | **`VERIFIES` is the only column that is substantially filled, and it is filled by one role — HR & Verification — which is also the role that cannot be paid from what it verifies.** One verifier across sixteen acts is a single point of failure and a segregation failure simultaneously |
| **GF-5** | **Founder compensation cannot be governed internally at all.** Proposer: nobody may. Approver: recused. Verifier: conflicted. **The only available auditor is external — which requires money, which returns to `H-Q17` and to `C_L1`** |
| **GF-6** | **Nothing in this matrix is resolved by this instrument.** Every `UNASSIGNED` is carried to §XX.4 as an open decision. **EA-24 prohibits silent resolution** |

## XX.4 The consolidated register of open governance decisions

**[P]** Every item below is `REQUIRES GOVERNANCE DECISION` unless another label is shown. **None may be resolved by an architecture document, by repetition, by an AI, or by the passage of time.**

| # | **Open decision** | **Where it arises** | **Additional label** |
|---|---|---|---|
| **`GD-01`** | **Who holds approval authority over a venture, a price, a payment, a reserve draw and a closure?** | **GF-1; XX.2** | **`REQUIRES FOUNDER DECISION` — and `SR-01` first** |
| **`GD-02`** | **Which of `C_L1-a`, `C_L1-b` or `C_L1-c` funds the L2 layer? (`H-Q17`)** | **XII.3** | **`REQUIRES FOUNDER DECISION` + `REQUIRES PROFESSIONAL VALIDATION`** |
| **`GD-03`** | **Who authorises a reserve or float draw, and on what trigger?** | **XIV.1, XIV.2, AG-11** | — |
| **`GD-04`** | **What is the unit of work for compensation — task, deliverable or hour?** | **XI.3** | — |
| **`GD-05`** | **Who may sign, given that no legal person exists?** | **GF-2** | **`BLOCKED — SR-01, A-N16`** |
| **`GD-06`** | **Does the organisation accept a funder, and what influence may that funder hold?** | **C_L1-a; Part XVI** | **`REQUIRES FOUNDER DECISION`** |
| **`GD-07`** | **Where does reinvestment sit in the priority order? (RI-1, RI-2 or RI-3)** | **X.7** | **Tier-1 amendment + `REQUIRES PROFESSIONAL VALIDATION`** |
| **`GD-08`** | **Does founder compensation remain at priority 4?** | **X.6, D-B** | **Tier-1 amendment + `REQUIRES PROFESSIONAL VALIDATION` + `REQUIRES FOUNDER DECISION`** |
| **`GD-09`** | **What are the concentration limit, the collection cycle and the coverage integer N?** | **RR-4, LC-2, XIV.3** | **`REQUIRES PROFESSIONAL VALIDATION` — no value may be invented** |
| **`GD-10`** | **Which of the four `D-8` roles cease to be volunteerable, and when?** | **XI.7 D-8; VB-5** | **`REQUIRES FOUNDER DECISION` + `REQUIRES PROFESSIONAL VALIDATION`** |
| **`GD-11`** | **Who performs the second pair of hands where only one person is available?** | **F-11; XIX.3 finding 4** | **`REQUIRES GOVERNANCE DECISION` — and it may be unanswerable at eleven people** |
| **`GD-12`** | **Who funds the governance, amendment, validation and audit programme itself?** | **XII.2 row 14; HC-10** | — |
| **`GD-13`** | **Is `MI-1` created, and from what source?** | **XIII.3; F-14** | **`REQUIRES PROFESSIONAL VALIDATION` on restricted-fund handling** |
| **`GD-14`** | **Who may amend the economic architecture, and by what route?** | **XX.2 final row** | **Tier-1: National + professional validation + founder's formal amendment** |
| **`GD-15`** | **Does the organisation accept that six failure modes are already open?** | **XVIII.4 finding 7** | **Acknowledgement, not decision — but it must be recorded by a human** |

# PART XXI — RECORDS AND DATA MODEL

## XXI.1 What this Part is, and the prohibition that governs it

**[P]** Bound by **EA-26** and **§18 of the delta-audit brief**. **This Part specifies a data model. It does not specify software.**

> ### **`NO APPLICATION, DASHBOARD, LEDGER SYSTEM, PAYMENT SYSTEM, IDENTITY SYSTEM, MEMBERSHIP PLATFORM, AUTOMATION OR AI SYSTEM IS DESIGNED, AUTHORISED OR IMPLIED BY THIS PART.`**
>
> **A record type is a statement of what must be true and knowable. It may be satisfied by a notebook, a spreadsheet, a paper register or a shared document. **The architecture requires the record, not the technology. Where a record cannot be kept because no means exists, that is `HC-12` and it is named as an unfunded requirement — not silently converted into a software project.***

| Design rule | **Content** |
|---|---|
| **DR-1** | **Every record carries all eight fields: `WHO → WHAT → WHEN → WHY → AMOUNT → AUTHORITY → EVIDENCE → STATUS`.** A record missing a field is incomplete, and **F-17** treats incompleteness as a failure, not as a draft |
| **DR-2** | **`AMOUNT` is a field even where the amount is zero, unknown or not yet determinable.** An empty amount and a zero amount are different facts and must be distinguishable |
| **DR-3** | **`AUTHORITY` names a person or a rule, never a system and never "the organisation".** Where no authority exists, the field reads **`UNASSIGNED`** — the honest state recorded in XX.2 |
| **DR-4** | **`EVIDENCE` names a primary source. A reference to a document is not the document** *(Rule K-5)*, and a receipt is not an acceptance *(Rule K-2)* |
| **DR-5** | **`STATUS` is drawn from a closed set so that a record cannot be quietly re-characterised:** `PROPOSED · APPROVED · EXECUTED · PAID · ACCRUED · SHORT · DISPUTED · REVERSED · CLOSED · VOID · UNVERIFIABLE` |
| **DR-6** | **No record may be amended in place.** A correction is a new record referencing the old, with the reason — **RT-16, AC-2, AG-13** |
| **DR-7** | **Every record is visible to the people it affects.** Compensation records to the person compensated; shortfall records to the team; contribution records to the members; mission records to the members and to any donor whose restriction is engaged |
| **DR-8** | **No record type may be used to compare people.** **AG-15** — the fields exist to establish facts, not to rank members |
| **DR-9** | **Records are retained for a period determined by law, not by convenience — and that period is `REQUIRES PROFESSIONAL VALIDATION`, because it depends on the entity form that does not yet exist** |

## XXI.2 The seventeen record types — REC-01 … REC-09

| ID | **Record type** | **WHO** | **WHAT** | **WHEN** | **WHY** | **AMOUNT** | **AUTHORITY** | **EVIDENCE** | **STATUS** |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **REC-01** | **Venture record** | The proposer and the approver | The category (one of seven), the scope, the intended customer, the cost structure, the collection cycle | At G1, and at every gate thereafter | Which gate authorised it, and under what rule | None — a venture record holds no money | **`UNASSIGNED` — GF-1** | The gate decision and its human signature | `PROPOSED → APPROVED → CLOSED / VOID` |
| **REC-02** | **Customer record** | The customer, the acquirer, the ultimate beneficiary | Identity, the acquisition source (**mission contact or not — AG-09**), the ultimate beneficiary (**AG-05**), the concentration share (**RR-4**) | On first contact and on every change | Which venture it belongs to and whether it is related (**RP-T1 … RP-T5**) | None | Team Leader within delegated authority | The acceptance and the source of the introduction | `PROPOSED → APPROVED → CLOSED` |
| **REC-03** | **Acceptance and delivery record** | The customer and the deliverer | What was delivered, what was accepted, on what terms, and what was not accepted | At delivery and at acceptance, separately | That revenue may only exist where value was received (**RR-6**) | The invoiced value, not the collected value | Ground-Level Manager | The customer's acceptance — **a receipt is not an acceptance (K-2)** | `EXECUTED → DISPUTED / CLOSED` |
| **REC-04** | **Revenue record** | The venture | `V_invoiced` and `V_collected` as **separate fields**, plus the gap between them (**RR-2**) | Per period, closed on a stated date | Which priority the money enters at | Both figures, always | Funds Manager — **custody, not approval** | Bank or cash reconciliation (**RT-18**) | `PROPOSED → EXECUTED → SHORT / CLOSED` |
| **REC-05** | **Cost record** | The person incurring it and the classifier | The cost, its **venture**, its **pool**, its **priority** and its **category** (**AG-02**) | When incurred, not when paid | Which layer it belongs to — L1, L2, L3 or L4 | The amount, or zero, or unknown (**DR-2**) | Funds Manager; **AI may flag the classification, a human decides it** | An invoice, a receipt or a stated absence of one | `PROPOSED → APPROVED → PAID / ACCRUED` |
| **REC-06** | **Work-verification record** | The worker, the verifier, and whether the verifier is paid from the same venture (**AG-01**) | The task, the deliverable, the hours, and the **mixed-work split where a role-holder performed more than one class of work (MW-1 … MW-6)** | On completion, before any payment | That priority 1 rests on verified work and nothing else (**EL-1, EL-2**) | The verified work value — no rate, no money | HR & Verification | The deliverable or the customer's confirmation | `PROPOSED → APPROVED → DISPUTED / UNVERIFIABLE` |
| **REC-07** | **Compensation formula record** | The author and the publisher | **The formula, its variables, its unit of work, its eligibility rule, its shortfall rule and its accrual rule — §XI.3** | Before the first person is recruited on it (**`D-1`**), and on every change | That a member can understand, see and challenge what they will be paid | **`ZERO — CONTAINS NO NUMBERS UNTIL APPROVED`** | **`REQUIRES FOUNDER DECISION` + National approval + `[V]`** | The published text and its effective date | `PROPOSED → APPROVED → VOID on supersession` |
| **REC-08** | **Payment sheet** | The payer, the payee, the calculator | Each person's entitlement, its components, and **the formula version that produced it (RT-16, AG-13)** | Per period | That priority 1 was discharged | The gross, the deduction and the net — **`REQUIRES PROFESSIONAL VALIDATION` as to the split (JT-2)** | **`UNASSIGNED — GF-1`**; signature **`BLOCKED — SR-01, A-N16`** | REC-06 plus the bank or cash record | `APPROVED → PAID / SHORT / ACCRUED` |
| **REC-09** | **Accrual record** | The person it belongs to | The earned, unpaid claim, its age, and the formula version that produced it (**AC-2**) | When the claim cannot be paid, in the same period | That a shortfall is a debt of record and not a disappointment (**AC-1 … AC-5**) | The accrued amount | The same authority as REC-08 | The shortfall computation and its publication | `ACCRUED → PAID / DISPUTED` — **never silently `CLOSED`** |

## XXI.3 The seventeen record types — REC-10 … REC-17

| ID | **Record type** | **WHO** | **WHAT** | **WHEN** | **WHY** | **AMOUNT** | **AUTHORITY** | **EVIDENCE** | **STATUS** |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **REC-10** | **Statutory obligation record** | The organisation and the authority owed | The obligation, its base, its rate and its due date — **each `REQUIRES PROFESSIONAL VALIDATION` until the entity form and worker classification exist** | When the obligation arises, before it is due | That the joint tier (**JT-1 … JT-5**) can be computed at all | The amount, or **`UNKNOWN`** | **`UNASSIGNED`**; signature **`BLOCKED — SR-01`** | The professional instruction relied on | `PROPOSED → PAID / SHORT` — **never reducible by any internal rule (JT-3)** |
| **REC-11** | **Pool and reserve record** | The proposer, the authoriser, the receiver | Which pool (**P-1 … P-5**), the trigger cited, the balance before and after, and the **return position** (**XIV.1, AG-11**) | On every contribution to and draw from a pool | That a reserve is a control and not a spare balance | Balance, draw and return | **`UNASSIGNED — GD-03`** | The trigger's evidence and the reconciliation | `APPROVED → EXECUTED → REVERSED on F-09` |
| **REC-12** | **Float record (P-6)** | The named source and the receiver | Source, cap, term, every draw, the reconciliation, and **the renewal count as an explicit field (MD-05, AG-12)** | At seeding and on every draw | That the first weak month has an answer other than a member's savings (**XIV.4**) | Cap, drawn, remaining, reconciled | The named source's terms | The seed agreement and the full reconciliation | `PROPOSED → EXECUTED → CLOSED on full reconciliation` — **`VOID` if renewed twice** |
| **REC-13** | **Contribution record** | The contributor and the receiver | Amount, purpose, restriction, and **an explicit field answering whether it creates any claim on the organisation, its revenue or its assets (`AG-10`, SEP-5)** | **Before acceptance**, not after | That funding never becomes influence by accident | The amount | **`UNASSIGNED — GD-01`**; **the contributor may not approve their own contribution** | The contributor's stated terms and the disclosure to members | `PROPOSED → APPROVED → EXECUTED / VOID` |
| **REC-14** | **Mission instrument record (`MI-1`)** | The authoriser, the receiver, the beneficiary population | The source, **the restriction as a field and not a footnote**, the use, and the service delivered | On every unit of mission money | That flow C and D remain separate from flow A (**X-1 … X-6**) and that a donor's intent is honoured | Amount in and amount out | **`UNASSIGNED — GD-13`** | The restriction's source and the service record | `APPROVED → EXECUTED → SHORT (F-14) / REVERSED (F-13)` |
| **REC-15** | **Related-party register** | Every person on both sides, and the recorder | The counterparty, **which of RP-T1 … RP-T5 makes it related**, the connection, the recusal, the arms-length evidence and the disclosure made | **Before the transaction** (**RP-1**) | That influence, not name, defines a related party (**AG-14**) | The amount, or **`ZERO`** where the transaction is non-monetary | **`UNASSIGNED — no disinterested approver exists (GF-5)`** | The recusal record and the comparable relied on | `PROPOSED → APPROVED / VOID (RP-8)` |
| **REC-16** | **Period reconciliation and solvency report** | The preparer and the reader | `V_invoiced`, `V_collected`, every priority's allocation, **and the highest solvency line reached — S-1 … S-6 (X.8)** | Every period, closed on a stated date | That the only honest answer to "how are we doing?" is a line, not a score (**AG-04, AG-15**) | Every priority's figure | Funds Manager prepares; **a human approves** | Bank or cash records (**RT-18**) | `EXECUTED` — **and it may not be reopened without a recorded reason** |
| **REC-17** | **Decision and authority record** | **The decider — always a named human** | The decision, the options considered, the reason, the rule relied on, **and where the decision was drafted or modelled by AI, a separate linked entry recording the AI output and the human decision apart from one another (F-18)** | At the moment of decision, including a decision not to decide | That authority is traceable, and that **the absence of a decision is itself a recorded fact (§XXIV.4)** | **`ZERO` — a decision record holds no money** | The named human and the rule | The gate, the recusal and any professional advice relied on | `PROPOSED → APPROVED → VOID / UNRESOLVED` |

## XXI.4 The completeness test

| Test | **Result** |
|---|---|
| **Do the 17 record types cover every economic act in XX.2?** | **`YES` — all sixteen acts produce at least one record, and every record type is produced by at least one act** |
| **Do they cover all 18 failure modes?** | **`YES` — each of F-01 … F-18 names its record in Part XVIII's `RECORD` column** |
| **Do they cover all 15 gaming vectors?** | **`YES` — each of AG-01 … AG-15 names its detection record in Part XIX** |
| **Do they satisfy the eight-field requirement of task §18?** | **`YES` — all 17 × 8 = 136 cells are filled, and none is left blank** |
| **Do they require software?** | **`NO` — §XXI.1's prohibition stands. DR-9's retention period is the only field that depends on a professional determination** |
| **How many `AUTHORITY` cells read `UNASSIGNED` or `BLOCKED`?** | **Nine of seventeen. **This is the honest measure of the architecture's readiness, and it agrees with GF-1 and GF-2 exactly***** |
| **How many cells contain a number?** | **`ZERO`** |

# PART XXII — AUDIT ARCHITECTURE

## XXII.1 What audit is for

**[P]** Bound by **RT-24, COI-2, EA-22**. **Audit exists to establish, after the fact and independently, whether what was recorded is what happened, and whether what happened was authorised. It does not exist to make the numbers look acceptable.**

| Audit is | **Audit is not** |
|---|---|
| **An examination of records against rules** | A certification of legality — **EA-26 prohibits any compliance declaration** |
| **A detection function** | **A cure, a clearance, a classification or a waiver** — F-17, F-18, RP-7 |
| **Independent of what it examines** | Independent of the people available — **COI-2 is a requirement the current team cannot satisfy** |
| **Able to say "unverifiable"** | Able to say "probably fine" — **an unverifiable transaction is treated as unauthorised (F-17)** |
| **Published to the members it concerns** | A report upward only — **DR-7** |

## XXII.2 The audit programme

| # | **Audit** | **Scope** | **Frequency** | **Performed by** | **Independence state** |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **AU-1** | **`T1` — the anti-MLM correlation report** | Every compensation record against every recruitment attribution. **One report; the entire anti-MLM assurance programme** **[S-C: §22.4]** | **Annual, and on any change to the compensation formula** | Internal Auditor | **`ACHIEVABLE — the data contains no personal judgement`** |
| **AU-2** | **`T4` — the participation-payment test** | Every receipt into every pool, tested for any money received from a participant for participation | **Annual, and on any new revenue mechanism** | Internal Auditor | **`ACHIEVABLE`** |
| **AU-3** | **Waterfall compliance** | Every period's allocation against the priority order and the joint tier | **Every period** | Internal Auditor | **`ACHIEVABLE — arithmetic against a fixed order`** |
| **AU-4** | **Reconciliation** | Every period's records against bank or cash | **Every period** | Internal Auditor, **not the Funds Manager** | **`AT RISK — COI-1 requires the Funds Manager to be outside it; with eleven people this may not be satisfiable`** |
| **AU-5** | **Verification independence** | Every REC-06, tested for whether the verifier is paid from the work verified | **Every period** | Internal Auditor | **`AT RISK — same constraint`** |
| **AU-6** | **Related-party register** | Every counterparty against RP-T1 … RP-T5, and every register entry against RP-2 and RP-4 | **Every period, and before every transaction** | Internal Auditor | **`AT RISK — the founder is a related party to everything`** |
| **AU-7** | **Reserve and float discipline** | Every draw against its trigger, authority and return; the float's renewal count | **Every draw, and annually in full** | Internal Auditor | **`ACHIEVABLE`** |
| **AU-8** | **Mission instrument and restriction compliance** | Every `MI-1` unit against its restriction, and every flow crossing against X-1 … X-8 | **Every period** | Internal Auditor | **`ACHIEVABLE`** |
| **AU-9** | **Record completeness** | All 17 record types against the eight fields; every `UNVERIFIABLE` status examined | **Every period** | Internal Auditor | **`ACHIEVABLE`** |
| **AU-10** | **Funding-map honesty** | Every cell of XII.2 against what actually happened, testing whether an unfunded mandate was performed anyway (**F-10**) | **Every period** | Internal Auditor | **`ACHIEVABLE — AND THE MOST IMPORTANT AUDIT IN THE PROGRAMME, BECAUSE IT IS THE ONLY ONE THAT DETECTS SILENT NON-COMPLIANCE`** |
| **AU-11** | **Founder compensation** | Priority 4 and priority 9 in full | **On every occurrence** | **`EXTERNAL ONLY`** | **`INTERNAL INDEPENDENCE IS NOT ACHIEVABLE — GF-5. Requires money, which returns to `H-Q17``** |
| **AU-12** | **AI boundary compliance** | Every economic act against Part VIII's matrix and Part XXIII's lists, testing whether any consequential act rests on an AI decision (**F-18**) | **Every period** | Internal Auditor, human | **`ACHIEVABLE`** |

## XXII.3 The audit findings that are already true

| # | **Finding** |
|---|---|
| **AF-1** | **Twelve audits are specified. Ten are achievable in principle; two are not achievable with the current team, and one of those two (AU-11) can never be internal** |
| **AF-2** | **Every audit currently has nothing to audit.** There is no revenue, no payment, no reserve, no draw, no mission instrument, no contribution and no record. **AU-1 … AU-12 all return empty today — and an empty audit is not a passed audit. It must be recorded as `NO POPULATION`, which is a different status from `NO EXCEPTIONS`** |
| **AF-3** | **AU-10 is the only audit that can produce a finding today**, because the funding map already contains eight `NONE` cells and the roles are already being performed. **F-10 is open, and it is auditable now** |
| **AF-4** | **The auditor is one of eleven people who also perform delivery work.** COI-2 requires that the auditor not be paid from what they audit; with no funding source for the role (XII.2 row 11) the requirement is satisfied only because nobody is paid at all. **That is compliance by poverty, and it will not survive the first collection** |
| **AF-5** | **Audit has no funding source (HC-10, GD-12) and no authority (GF-1).** An audit function that can detect everything and compel nothing is a diary |
| **AF-6** | **No audit in this programme requires software.** AU-1 and AU-2 are two queries over records that DR-1 … DR-9 already require |
